shuffle_result[15:8] : operand_c_i[15:8];\n  assign pack_result[7:0] = shuffle_through[0] ? shuffle_result[7:0] : operand_c_i[7:0];\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Bit counting\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic [31:0] ff_input;\n  logic [ 5:0] cnt_result;\n  logic [ 5:0] clb_result;\n  logic [ 4:0] ff1_result;\n  logic        ff_no_one;\n  logic [ 4:0] fl1_result;\n  logic [ 5:0] bitop_result;\n\n  cv32e40p_popcnt popcnt_i (\n      .in_i    (operand_a_i),\n      .result_o(cnt_result)\n  );\n\n  always_comb begin\n    ff_input = '0;\n    case (operator_i)\n      ALU_FF1: ff_input = operand_a_i;\n      ALU_DIVU, ALU_REMU, ALU_FL1: ff_input = operand_a_rev;\n      ALU_DIV, ALU_REM, ALU_CLB: begin\n        if (operand_a_i[31]) ff_input = operand_a_neg_rev;\n        else ff_input = operand_a_rev;\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  cv32e40p_ff_one ff_one_i (\n      .in_i       (ff_input),\n      .first_one_o(ff1_result),\n      .no_ones_o  (ff_no_one)\n  );\n\n  assign fl1_result = 5'd31 - ff1_result;\n  assign clb_result = ff1_result - 5'd1;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    bitop_result = '0;\n    case (operator_i)\n      ALU_FF1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_result};\n      ALU_FL1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, fl1_result};\n      ALU_CNT: bitop_result = cnt_result;\n      ALU_CLB: begin\n        if (ff_no_one) begin\n          if (operand_a_i[31]) bitop_result = 6'd31;\n          else bitop_result = '0;\n        end else begin\n          bitop_result = clb_result;\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Bit manipulation\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic extract_is_signed;\n  logic extract_sign;\n  logic [31:0] bmask_first, bmask_inv;\n  logic [31:0] bextins_and;\n  logic [31:0] bextins_result, bclr_result, bset_result;\n\n  assign bmask_first       = {32'hFFFFFFFE} << bmask_a_i;\n  assign bmask             = (~bmask_first) << bmask_b_i;\n  assign bmask_inv         = ~bmask;\n\n  assign bextins_and       = (operator_i == ALU_BINS) ? operand_c_i : {32{extract_sign}};\n\n  assign extract_is_signed = (operator_i == ALU_BEXT);\n  assign extract_sign      = extract_is_signed & shift_result[bmask_a_i];\n\n  assign bextins_result    = (bmask & shift_result) | (bextins_and & bmask_inv);\n\n  assign bclr_result       = operand_a_i & bmask_inv;\n  assign bset_result       = operand_a_i | bmask;\n\n  // bit reverse\n  logic [31:0] radix_2_rev;\n  logic [31:0] radix_4_rev;\n  logic [31:0] radix_8_rev;\n  logic [31:0] reverse_result;\n  logic [ 1:0] radix_mux_sel;\n\n  assign radix_mux_sel = bmask_a_i[1:0];\n\n  generate\n    for (j = 0; j < 32; j++) begin : gen_radix_2_rev\n      assign radix_2_rev[j] = shift_result[31-j];\n    end\n    for (j = 0; j < 16; j++) begin : gen_radix_4_rev\n      assign radix_4_rev[2*j+1:2*j] = shift_result[31-j*2:31-j*2-1];\n    end\n    for (j = 0; j < 10; j++) begin : gen_radix_8_rev\n      assign radix_8_rev[3*j+2:3*j] = shift_result[31-j*3:31-j*3-2];\n    end\n    assign radix_8_rev[31:30] = 2'b0;\n  endgenerate\n\n  always_comb begin\n    reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n    unique case (radix_mux_sel)\n      2'b00: reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n      2'b01: reverse_result = radix_4_rev;\n      2'b10: reverse_result = radix_8_rev;\n      default: reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Divider\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  logic        div_signed;\n  logic        div_op_a_signed;\n  logic [ 5:0] div_shift_int;\n\n  assign div_signed = operator_i[0];\n\n  assign div_op_a_signed = operand_a_i[31] & div_signed;\n\n  assign div_shift_int = ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result;\n  assign div_shift = div_shift_int + (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n\n  assign div_valid = enable_i & (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01100);\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI(rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (shift_left_result),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(~|operand_a_i),\n      .OpBSign_SI(div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO(result_div),\n      .InVld_SI (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO(div_ready)\n  );\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Result mux: one-hot AND-OR\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic g_and, g_or, g_xor, g_addsh, g_bextins, g_bclr, g_bset, g_brev;\n  logic g_pack, g_minmax, g_abs, g_clip, g_vcmp, g_slt, g_bitcnt, g_divop;\n\n  assign g_and    = (operator_i == ALU_AND);\n  assign g_or     = (operator_i == ALU_OR);\n  assign g_xor    = (operator_i == ALU_XOR);\n  assign g_addsh  = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0011) || (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01001);\n  assign g_bextins = (operator_i == ALU_BEXT) || (operator_i == ALU_BEXTU) || (operator_i == ALU_BINS);\n  assign g_bclr   = (operator_i == ALU_BCLR);\n  assign g_bset   = (operator_i == ALU_BSET);\n  assign g_brev   = (operator_i == ALU_BREV);\n  assign g_pack   = (operator_i == ALU_SHUF) || (operator_i == ALU_SHUF2) ||\n                    (operator_i == ALU_PCKLO) || (operator_i == ALU_PCKHI) ||\n                    (operator_i == ALU_EXT) || (operator_i == ALU_EXTS) || (operator_i == ALU_INS);\n  assign g_minmax = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00100);  // MIN MINU MAX MAXU\n  assign g_abs    = (operator_i == ALU_ABS);\n  assign g_clip   = (operator_i == ALU_CLIP) || (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU);\n  assign g_vcmp   = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0000 && operator_i[1] == 1'b0) ||\n                    (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0001);   // vector compares incl EQ/NE\n  assign g_slt    = (operator_i == ALU_SLTS) || (operator_i == ALU_SLTU) ||\n                    (operator_i == ALU_SLETS) || (operator_i == ALU_SLETU);\n  assign g_bitcnt = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01101);  // CNT CLB FF1 FL1\n  assign g_divop  = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01100);\n\n  logic [31:0] abs_result;\n  assign abs_result = is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]} : result_minmax;\n\n  logic [31:0] cmp_fill;\n  assign cmp_fill = {{8{cmp_result[3]}}, {8{cmp_result[2]}}, {8{cmp_result[1]}}, {8{cmp_result[0]}}};\n\n  assign result_o = ({32{g_and}} & (operand_a_i & operand_b_i))\n                  | ({32{g_or}}  & (operand_a_i | operand_b_i))\n                  | ({32{g_xor}} & (operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i))\n                  | ({32{g_addsh}} & shift_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bextins}} & bextins_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bclr}} & bclr_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bset}} & bset_result)\n                  | ({32{g_brev}} & reverse_result)\n                  | ({32{g_pack}} & pack_result)\n                  | ({32{g_minmax}} & result_minmax)\n                  | ({32{g_abs}} & abs_result)\n                  | ({32{g_clip}} & clip_result)\n                  | ({32{g_vcmp}} & cmp_fill)\n                  | ({32{g_slt}} & {31'b0, cmp_result[3]})\n                  | ({32{g_bitcnt}} & {26'h0, bitop_result})\n                  | ({32{g_divop}} & result_div);\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---- popcount (same as upstream) ----\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  generate\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  generate\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  generate\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\nendmodule\n\n// ---- find first one (same as upstream) ----\nmodule cv32e40p_ff_one #(\n    parameter LEN = 32\n) (\n    input logic [LEN-1:0] in_i,\n    output logic [$clog2(LEN)-1:0] first_one_o,\n    output logic                   no_ones_o\n);\n  localparam NUM_LEVELS = $clog2(LEN);\n  logic [          LEN-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_lut;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0]                 sel_nodes;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_nodes;\n\n  generate\n    genvar j;\n    for (j = 0; j < LEN; j++) begin : gen_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[j] = $unsigned(j);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    genvar l;\n    genvar level;\n    assign sel_nodes[2**NUM_LEVELS-1] = 1'b0;\n    for (level = 0; level < NUM_LEVELS; level++) begin : gen_tree\n      if (level < NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_non_root_level\n        for (l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : gen_node\n          assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+l]   = sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] | sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];\n          assign index_nodes[2**level-1+l] = (sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] == 1'b1) ?\n                                           index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] : index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];\n        end\n      end\n      if (level == NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_root_level\n        for (genvar kk = 0; kk < 2 ** level; kk++) begin : gen_node\n          if (kk * 2 < LEN - 1) begin : gen_two\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+kk] = in_i[kk*2] | in_i[kk*2+1];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+kk] = (in_i[kk*2] == 1'b1) ? index_lut[kk*2] : index_lut[kk*2+1];\n          end\n          if (kk * 2 == LEN - 1) begin : gen_one\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+kk]   = in_i[kk*2];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+kk] = index_lut[kk*2];\n          end\n          if (kk * 2 > LEN - 1) begin : gen_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+kk]   = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+kk] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign first_one_o = index_nodes[0];\n  assign no_ones_o   = ~sel_nodes[0];\nendmodule\n\n// ---- serial divider (same as upstream) ----\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter BMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] OpCode_SI[1] : RemSel_SP;\n  assign CompInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBSign_SI : CompInv_SP;\n  assign ResInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? (~OpBIsZero_SI | OpCode_SI[1]) & OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(\n      OpA_DI\n  )] ^ OpBSign_SI) : ResInv_SP;\n\n  assign AReg_DN = (ARegEn_S) ? AddOut_D : AReg_DP;\n  assign BReg_DN = (BRegEn_S) ? BMux_D : BReg_DP;\n  assign ResReg_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? '0 : (ResRegEn_S) ? {\n    ABComp_S, ResReg_DP[$high(ResReg_DP):1]\n  } : ResReg_DP;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge Clk_CI or negedge Rst_RBI) begin : p_regs\n    if (~Rst_RBI) begin\n      State_SP   <= IDLE;\n      AReg_DP    <= '0;\n      BReg_DP    <= '0;\n      ResReg_DP  <= '0;\n      Cnt_DP     <= '0;\n      RemSel_SP  <= 1'b0;\n      CompInv_SP <= 1'b0;\n      ResInv_SP  <= 1'b0;\n    end else begin\n      State_SP   <= State_SN;\n      AReg_DP    <= AReg_DN;\n      BReg_DP    <= BReg_DN;\n      ResReg_DP  <= ResReg_DN;\n      Cnt_DP     <= Cnt_DN;\n      RemSel_SP  <= RemSel_SN;\n      CompInv_SP <= CompInv_SN;\n      ResInv_SP  <= ResInv_SN;\n    end\n  end\nendmodule\n")

Best so far: 606489.4874916598 area_delay_product (eval 2)

[ASSISTANT]
v1 passed at 606489 (6.4% improvement). The critical path runs through the operator decode → shared barrel shifter → final mux. Main structural insight: the shifter is shared between the operand-fed operations (shift/bit manipulation/division prep) and the adder-fed operations (the ADD family that requires adder→round→shift). By splitting the shifter into two, we remove the operator-mux chain from the worst path.

Let's create v2 with a split shifter:

operand_a_i[23:16] : minmax_b[23:16];\n  assign result_minmax[15:8] = (sel_minmax[1] == 1'b1) ? operand_a_i[15:8] : minmax_b[15:8];\n  assign result_minmax[7:0] = (sel_minmax[0] == 1'b1) ? operand_a_i[7:0] : minmax_b[7:0];\n\n  // clip\n  logic [31:0] clip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    clip_result = result_minmax;\n    if (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) begin\n      if (operand_a_i[31] || is_equal_clip) begin\n        clip_result = '0;\n      end else begin\n        clip_result = result_minmax;\n      end\n    end else begin\n      if (adder_result_expanded[36] || is_equal_clip) begin\n        clip_result = operand_b_neg;\n      end else begin\n        clip_result = result_minmax;\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Shuffle / pack\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic [3:0][1:0] shuffle_byte_sel;\n  logic [3:0]      shuffle_reg_sel;\n  logic [1:0]      shuffle_reg1_sel;\n  logic [1:0]      shuffle_reg0_sel;\n  logic [3:0]      shuffle_through;\n\n  logic [31:0] shuffle_r1, shuffle_r0;\n  logic [31:0] shuffle_r1_in, shuffle_r0_in;\n  logic [31:0] shuffle_result;\n  logic [31:0] pack_result;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    shuffle_reg_sel  = '0;\n    shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b01;\n    shuffle_reg0_sel = 2'b10;\n    shuffle_through  = '1;\n\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS: begin\n        if (operator_i == ALU_EXTS) shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b11;\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel[3:1] = 3'b111;\n          shuffle_reg_sel[0]   = 1'b0;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel[3:2] = 2'b11;\n          shuffle_reg_sel[1:0] = 2'b00;\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_PCKLO: begin\n        shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b00;\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          shuffle_through = 4'b0011;\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0001;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0011;\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_PCKHI: begin\n        shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b00;\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          shuffle_through = 4'b1100;\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0100;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0011;\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_SHUF2: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~operand_b_i[26];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~operand_b_i[18];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] = ~operand_b_i[10];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] = ~operand_b_i[2];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~operand_b_i[17];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~operand_b_i[17];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] = ~operand_b_i[1];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] = ~operand_b_i[1];\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_reg0_sel = 2'b00;\n            unique case (imm_vec_ext_i)\n              2'b00: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1110;\n              2'b01: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1101;\n              2'b10: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1011;\n              2'b11: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b0111;\n            endcase\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_reg0_sel   = 2'b01;\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] = imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] = imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  always_comb begin\n    shuffle_byte_sel = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_EXTS, ALU_EXT: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b0};\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      ALU_PCKLO: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: ;\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b01;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b00;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b01;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b00;\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      ALU_PCKHI: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: ;\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b11;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b10;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b11;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b10;\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      ALU_SHUF2, ALU_SHUF: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = operand_b_i[25:24];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = operand_b_i[17:16];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = operand_b_i[9:8];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = operand_b_i[1:0];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = {operand_b_i[16], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = {operand_b_i[16], 1'b0};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = {operand_b_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = {operand_b_i[0], 1'b0};\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b11;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b10;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b01;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b00;\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign shuffle_r0_in = shuffle_reg0_sel[1] ?\n                          operand_a_i :\n                          (shuffle_reg0_sel[0] ? {2{operand_a_i[15:0]}} : {4{operand_a_i[7:0]}});\n\n  assign shuffle_r1_in = shuffle_reg1_sel[1] ? {\n    {8{operand_a_i[31]}}, {8{operand_a_i[23]}}, {8{operand_a_i[15]}}, {8{operand_a_i[7]}}\n  } : (shuffle_reg1_sel[0] ? operand_c_i : operand_b_i);\n\n  assign shuffle_r0[31:24] = shuffle_byte_sel[3][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r0[23:16] = shuffle_byte_sel[2][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r0[15: 8] = shuffle_byte_sel[1][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r0[ 7:0] = shuffle_byte_sel[0][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n\n  assign shuffle_r1[31:24] = shuffle_byte_sel[3][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r1[23:16] = shuffle_byte_sel[2][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r1[15: 8] = shuffle_byte_sel[1][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r1[ 7:0] = shuffle_byte_sel[0][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n\n  assign shuffle_result[31:24] = shuffle_reg_sel[3] ? shuffle_r1[31:24] : shuffle_r0[31:24];\n  assign shuffle_result[23:16] = shuffle_reg_sel[2] ? shuffle_r1[23:16] : shuffle_r0[23:16];\n  assign shuffle_result[15:8] = shuffle_reg_sel[1] ? shuffle_r1[15:8] : shuffle_r0[15:8];\n  assign shuffle_result[7:0] = shuffle_reg_sel[0] ? shuffle_r1[7:0] : shuffle_r0[7:0];\n\n  assign pack_result[31:24] = shuffle_through[3] ? shuffle_result[31:24] : operand_c_i[31:24];\n  assign pack_result[23:16] = shuffle_through[2] ? shuffle_result[23:16] : operand_c_i[23:16];\n  assign pack_result[15:8] = shuffle_through[1] ? shuffle_result[15:8] : operand_c_i[15:8];\n  assign pack_result[7:0] = shuffle_through[0] ? shuffle_result[7:0] : operand_c_i[7:0];\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Bit counting\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic [31:0] ff_input;\n  logic [ 5:0] cnt_result;\n  logic [ 5:0] clb_result;\n  logic [ 4:0] ff1_result;\n  logic        ff_no_one;\n  logic [ 4:0] fl1_result;\n  logic [ 5:0] bitop_result;\n  logic        a_is_zero;\n\n  assign a_is_zero = ~|operand_a_i;\n\n  cv32e40p_popcnt popcnt_i (\n      .in_i    (operand_a_i),\n      .result_o(cnt_result)\n  );\n\n  always_comb begin\n    ff_input = '0;\n    case (operator_i)\n      ALU_FF1: ff_input = operand_a_i;\n      ALU_DIVU, ALU_REMU, ALU_FL1: ff_input = operand_a_rev;\n      ALU_DIV, ALU_REM, ALU_CLB: begin\n        if (operand_a_i[31]) ff_input = ~operand_a_rev;\n        else ff_input = operand_a_rev;\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  cv32e40p_ff_one ff_one_i (\n      .in_i       (ff_input),\n      .first_one_o(ff1_result),\n      .no_ones_o  (ff_no_one)\n  );\n\n  assign fl1_result = 5'd31 - ff1_result;\n  assign clb_result = ff1_result - 5'd1;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    bitop_result = '0;\n    case (operator_i)\n      ALU_FF1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_result};\n      ALU_FL1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, fl1_result};\n      ALU_CNT: bitop_result = cnt_result;\n      ALU_CLB: begin\n        if (ff_no_one) begin\n          if (operand_a_i[31]) bitop_result = 6'd31;\n          else bitop_result = '0;\n        end else begin\n          bitop_result = clb_result;\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Bit manipulation\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic extract_is_signed;\n  logic extract_sign;\n  logic [31:0] bmask_first, bmask_inv;\n  logic [31:0] bextins_and;\n  logic [31:0] bextins_result, bclr_result, bset_result;\n\n  assign bmask_first       = {32'hFFFFFFFE} << bmask_a_i;\n  assign bmask             = (~bmask_first) << bmask_b_i;\n  assign bmask_inv         = ~bmask;\n\n  assign bextins_and       = (operator_i == ALU_BINS) ? operand_c_i : {32{extract_sign}};\n\n  assign extract_is_signed = (operator_i == ALU_BEXT);\n  assign extract_sign      = extract_is_signed & shA_result[bmask_a_i];\n\n  assign bextins_result    = (bmask & shA_result) | (bextins_and & bmask_inv);\n\n  assign bclr_result       = operand_a_i & bmask_inv;\n  assign bset_result       = operand_a_i | bmask;\n\n  // bit reverse\n  logic [31:0] radix_2_rev;\n  logic [31:0] radix_4_rev;\n  logic [31:0] radix_8_rev;\n  logic [31:0] reverse_result;\n\n  generate\n    for (j = 0; j < 32; j++) begin : gen_radix_2_rev\n      assign radix_2_rev[j] = shA_result[31-j];\n    end\n    for (j = 0; j < 16; j++) begin : gen_radix_4_rev\n      assign radix_4_rev[2*j+1:2*j] = shA_result[31-j*2:31-j*2-1];\n    end\n    for (j = 0; j < 10; j++) begin : gen_radix_8_rev\n      assign radix_8_rev[3*j+2:3*j] = shA_result[31-j*3:31-j*3-2];\n    end\n    assign radix_8_rev[31:30] = 2'b0;\n  endgenerate\n\n  always_comb begin\n    reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n    unique case (bmask_a_i[1:0])\n      2'b01: reverse_result = radix_4_rev;\n      2'b10: reverse_result = radix_8_rev;\n      default: reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Divider\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  logic        div_signed;\n  logic        div_op_a_signed;\n  logic [ 5:0] div_shift_int;\n\n  assign div_signed = operator_i[0];\n\n  assign div_op_a_signed = operand_a_i[31] & div_signed;\n\n  assign div_shift_int = ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result;\n  assign div_shift = div_shift_int + (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI(rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (shA_leftres),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(a_is_zero),\n      .OpBSign_SI(div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO(result_div),\n      .InVld_SI (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO(div_ready)\n  );\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Result mux: one-hot AND-OR\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic g_and, g_or, g_xor, g_addfam, g_shift, g_bextins, g_bclr, g_bset, g_brev;\n  logic g_pack, g_minmax, g_abs, g_clip, g_vcmp, g_slt, g_bitcnt;\n\n  assign g_and    = (operator_i == ALU_AND);\n  assign g_or     = (operator_i == ALU_OR);\n  assign g_xor    = (operator_i == ALU_XOR);\n  assign g_addfam = is_addfam;\n  assign g_shift  = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01001);\n  assign g_bextins = (operator_i == ALU_BEXT) || (operator_i == ALU_BEXTU) || (operator_i == ALU_BINS);\n  assign g_bclr   = (operator_i == ALU_BCLR);\n  assign g_bset   = (operator_i == ALU_BSET);\n  assign g_brev   = (operator_i == ALU_BREV);\n  assign g_pack   = (operator_i == ALU_SHUF) || (operator_i == ALU_SHUF2) ||\n                    (operator_i == ALU_PCKLO) || (operator_i == ALU_PCKHI) ||\n                    (operator_i == ALU_EXT) || (operator_i == ALU_EXTS) || (operator_i == ALU_INS);\n  assign g_minmax = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00100);  // MIN MINU MAX MAXU\n  assign g_abs    = (operator_i == ALU_ABS);\n  assign g_clip   = (operator_i == ALU_CLIP) || (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU);\n  assign g_vcmp   = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0000 && operator_i[1] == 1'b0) ||\n                    (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0001);\n  assign g_slt    = (operator_i == ALU_SLTS) || (operator_i == ALU_SLTU) ||\n                    (operator_i == ALU_SLETS) || (operator_i == ALU_SLETU);\n  assign g_bitcnt = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01101);  // CNT CLB FF1 FL1\n\n  logic [31:0] abs_result;\n  assign abs_result = is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]} : result_minmax;\n\n  logic [31:0] cmp_fill;\n  assign cmp_fill = {{8{cmp_result[3]}}, {8{cmp_result[2]}}, {8{cmp_result[1]}}, {8{cmp_result[0]}}};\n\n  assign result_o = ({32{g_and}} & (operand_a_i & operand_b_i))\n                  | ({32{g_or}}  & (operand_a_i | operand_b_i))\n                  | ({32{g_xor}} & (operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i))\n                  | ({32{g_addfam}} & shB_right)\n                  | ({32{g_shift}} & shA_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bextins}} & bextins_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bclr}} & bclr_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bset}} & bset_result)\n                  | ({32{g_brev}} & reverse_result)\n                  | ({32{g_pack}} & pack_result)\n                  | ({32{g_minmax}} & result_minmax)\n                  | ({32{g_abs}} & abs_result)\n                  | ({32{g_clip}} & clip_result)\n                  | ({32{g_vcmp}} & cmp_fill)\n                  | ({32{g_slt}} & {31'b0, cmp_result[3]})\n                  | ({32{g_bitcnt}} & {26'h0, bitop_result})\n                  | ({32{is_divop}} & result_div);\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---- popcount ----\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  generate\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  generate\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  generate\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\nendmodule\n\n// ---- find first one ----\nmodule cv32e40p_ff_one #(\n    parameter LEN = 32\n) (\n    input logic [LEN-1:0] in_i,\n    output logic [$clog2(LEN)-1:0] first_one_o,\n    output logic                   no_ones_o\n);\n  localparam NUM_LEVELS = $clog2(LEN);\n  logic [          LEN-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_lut;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0]                 sel_nodes;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_nodes;\n\n  generate\n    genvar j;\n    for (j = 0; j < LEN; j++) begin : gen_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[j] = $unsigned(j);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    genvar l;\n    genvar level;\n    assign sel_nodes[2**NUM_LEVELS-1] = 1'b0;\n    for (level = 0; level < NUM_LEVELS; level++) begin : gen_tree\n      if (level < NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_non_root_level\n        for (l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : gen_node\n          assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+l]   = sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] | sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];\n          assign index_nodes[2**level-1+l] = (sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] == 1'b1) ?\n                                           index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] : index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];\n        end\n      end\n      if (level == NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_root_level\n        for (genvar kk = 0; kk < 2 ** level; kk++) begin : gen_node\n          if (kk * 2 < LEN - 1) begin : gen_two\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+kk] = in_i[kk*2] | in_i[kk*2+1];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+kk] = (in_i[kk*2] == 1'b1) ? index_lut[kk*2] : index_lut[kk*2+1];\n          end\n          if (kk * 2 == LEN - 1) begin : gen_one\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+kk]   = in_i[kk*2];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+kk] = index_lut[kk*2];\n          end\n          if (kk * 2 > LEN - 1) begin : gen_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+kk]   = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+kk] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign first_one_o = index_nodes[0];\n  assign no_ones_o   = ~sel_nodes[0];\nendmodule\n\n// ---- serial divider ----\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,\n    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,\n    input  logic                   InVld_SI,\n    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,\n    output logic                   OutVld_SO,\n    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO\n);\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n\n  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;\n  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;\n  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddTmp_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {\n    IDLE,\n    DIVIDE,\n    FINISH\n  }\n      State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpA_DI : BReg_DP;\n  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? AReg_DP : ResReg_DP_rev;\n  assign Res_DO = (ResInv_SP) ? -$signed(OutMux_D) : OutMux_D;\n  assign ABComp_S    = ((AReg_DP == BReg_DP) | ((AReg_DP > BReg_DP) ^ CompInv_SP)) & ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);\n  assign AddTmp_D = (LoadEn_S) ? 0 : AReg_DP;\n  assign AddOut_D = (PmSel_S) ? AddTmp_D + AddMux_D : AddTmp_D - $signed(AddMux_D);\n\n  assign Cnt_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBShift_DI : (~CntZero_S) ? Cnt_DP - 1 : Cnt_DP;\n  assign CntZero_S = ~(|Cnt_DP);\n\n  always_comb begin : p_fsm\n    State_SN   = State_SP;\n    OutVld_SO  = 1'b0;\n    LoadEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ARegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    BRegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ResRegEn_S = 1'b0;\n\n    case (State_SP)\n      IDLE: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (InVld_SI) begin\n          OutVld_SO = 1'b0;\n          ARegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          BRegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          LoadEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          State_SN  = DIVIDE;\n        end\n      end\n      DIVIDE: begin\n        ARegEn_S   = ABComp_S;\n        BRegEn_S   = 1'b1;\n        ResRegEn_S = 1'b1;\n        if (CntZero_S) begin\n          State_SN = FINISH;\n        end\n      end\n      FINISH: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (OutRdy_SI) begin\n          State_SN = IDLE;\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign RemSel_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpCode_SI[1] : RemSel_SP;\n  assign CompInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBSign_SI : CompInv_SP;\n  assign ResInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? (~OpBIsZero_SI | OpCode_SI[1]) & OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(\n      OpA_DI\n  )] ^ OpBSign_SI) : ResInv_SP;\n\n  assign AReg_DN = (ARegEn_S) ? AddOut_D : AReg_DP;\n  assign BReg_DN = (BRegEn_S) ? BMux_D : BReg_DP;\n  assign ResReg_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? '0 : (ResRegEn_S) ? {\n    ABComp_S, ResReg_DP[$high(ResReg_DP):1]\n  } : ResReg_DP;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge Clk_CI or negedge Rst_RBI) begin : p_regs\n    if (~Rst_RBI) begin\n      State_SP   <= IDLE;\n      AReg_DP    <= '0;\n      BReg_DP    <= '0;\n      ResReg_DP  <= '0;\n      Cnt_DP     <= '0;\n      RemSel_SP  <= 1'b0;\n      CompInv_SP <= 1'b0;\n      ResInv_SP  <= 1'b0;\n    end else begin\n      State_SP   <= State_SN;\n      AReg_DP    <= AReg_DN;\n      BReg_DP    <= BReg_DN;\n      ResReg_DP  <= ResReg_DN;\n      Cnt_DP     <= Cnt_DN;\n      RemSel_SP  <= RemSel_SN;\n      CompInv_SP <= CompInv_SN;\n      ResInv_SP  <= ResInv_SN;\n    end\n  end\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v2.sv')

Big improvement: 533803 (down from 606489). The remaining critical path is operator decode → adder operand muxes → 36-bit segmented adder → 32-bit round adder → shift → final OR.

Next, v3:
1. Remove ABS/CLIP from the shared segmented adder (each has its own dedicated adder), and remove subrot (two 16-bit subtractors) — this way the adder operand mux disappears (`op_a = a` directly).
2. In 32-bit mode (where the separator bit is transparent to carry), merge the rounding addition into the first-stage addition as a 3-operand addition.

//////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic        shA_left;\n  logic        shA_arith;\n  logic [31:0] shA_amt, shA_amt_left, shA_amt_int, shA_op;\n  logic [31:0] shA_right, shA_leftres, shA_result;\n  logic [63:0] shA_op64;\n\n  assign shA_left = (operator_i == ALU_SLL) || (operator_i == ALU_BINS) ||\n                    (operator_i == ALU_BREV) || is_divop;\n  assign shA_arith = (operator_i == ALU_SRA) || (operator_i == ALU_BEXT);\n\n  assign shA_op = shA_left ? operand_a_rev : operand_a_i;\n  assign shA_amt = div_valid ? {26'b0, div_shift} : operand_b_i;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        shA_amt_left[15:0]  = shA_amt[31:16];\n        shA_amt_left[31:16] = shA_amt[15:0];\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        shA_amt_left[7:0]   = shA_amt[31:24];\n        shA_amt_left[15:8]  = shA_amt[23:16];\n        shA_amt_left[23:16] = shA_amt[15:8];\n        shA_amt_left[31:24] = shA_amt[7:0];\n      end\n      default: begin\n        shA_amt_left[31:0] = shA_amt[31:0];\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign shA_amt_int = shA_left ? shA_amt_left : shA_amt;\n\n  assign shA_op64 = (operator_i == ALU_ROR) ? {shA_op, shA_op} :\n                    {{32{shA_arith & shA_op[31]}}, shA_op};\n\n  always_comb begin\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        shA_right[31:16] = $signed({shA_arith & shA_op[31], shA_op[31:16]}) >>> shA_amt_int[19:16];\n        shA_right[15:0]  = $signed({shA_arith & shA_op[15], shA_op[15:0]}) >>> shA_amt_int[3:0];\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        shA_right[31:24] = $signed({shA_arith & shA_op[31], shA_op[31:24]}) >>> shA_amt_int[26:24];\n        shA_right[23:16] = $signed({shA_arith & shA_op[23], shA_op[23:16]}) >>> shA_amt_int[18:16];\n        shA_right[15:8]  = $signed({shA_arith & shA_op[15], shA_op[15:8]}) >>> shA_amt_int[10:8];\n        shA_right[7:0]   = $signed({shA_arith & shA_op[7], shA_op[7:0]}) >>> shA_amt_int[2:0];\n      end\n      default: begin\n        shA_right = shA_op64 >> shA_amt_int[4:0];\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  genvar j;\n  generate\n    for (j = 0; j < 32; j++) begin : gen_shA_left_result\n      assign shA_leftres[j] = shA_right[31-j];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign shA_result = shA_left ? shA_leftres : shA_right;\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Shifter B: adder-fed (adder family right shift)\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic        shB_arith;\n  logic [4:0]  shB_amt;\n  logic [31:0] shB_right;\n  logic [63:0] shB_op64;\n\n  assign shB_arith = ~operator_i[1];  // ADD/SUB/ADDR/SUBR arith, U variants logical\n  assign shB_amt = is_clpx_i ? {3'b000, clpx_shift_i} : bmask_b_i;\n\n  assign shB_op64 = {{32{shB_arith & adder_round_result[31]}}, adder_round_result};\n\n  always_comb begin\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        shB_right[31:16] = $signed({shB_arith & adder_round_result[31], adder_round_result[31:16]}) >>> shB_amt[3:0];\n        shB_right[15:0]  = $signed({shB_arith & adder_round_result[15], adder_round_result[15:0]}) >>> shB_amt[3:0];\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        shB_right[31:24] = $signed({shB_arith & adder_round_result[31], adder_round_result[31:24]}) >>> shB_amt[2:0];\n        shB_right[23:16] = $signed({shB_arith & adder_round_result[23], adder_round_result[23:16]}) >>> shB_amt[2:0];\n        shB_right[15:8]  = $signed({shB_arith & adder_round_result[15], adder_round_result[15:8]}) >>> shB_amt[2:0];\n        shB_right[7:0]   = $signed({shB_arith & adder_round_result[7], adder_round_result[7:0]}) >>> shB_amt[2:0];\n      end\n      default: begin\n        shB_right = shB_op64 >> shB_amt[4:0];\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Comparisons\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic [ 3:0] is_equal;\n  logic [ 3:0] is_greater;\n\n  logic [ 3:0] cmp_signed;\n  logic [ 3:0] is_equal_vec;\n  logic [ 3:0] is_greater_vec;\n  logic [31:0] operand_b_eq;\n  logic        is_equal_clip;\n\n  assign operand_b_eq = (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) ? '0 : operand_b_neg;\n  assign is_equal_clip = operand_a_i == operand_b_eq;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    cmp_signed = 4'b0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_SLTS,\n      ALU_SLETS,\n      ALU_ABS,\n      ALU_CLIP,\n      ALU_CLIPU: begin\n        case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8:  cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1111;\n          VEC_MODE16: cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1010;\n          default:    cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1000;\n        endcase\n      end\n      default:\n        if (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0000 && !operator_i[1] && operator_i[2])  // GTS GES LTS LES\n          case (vector_mode_i)\n            VEC_MODE8:  cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1111;\n            VEC_MODE16: cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1010;\n            default:    cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1000;\n          endcase\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : gen_is_vec\n      assign is_equal_vec[i] = (operand_a_i[8*i+7:8*i] == operand_b_i[8*i+7:i*8]);\n      assign is_greater_vec[i] = $signed(\n          {operand_a_i[8*i+7] & cmp_signed[i], operand_a_i[8*i+7:8*i]}\n      ) > $signed(\n          {operand_b_i[8*i+7] & cmp_signed[i], operand_b_i[8*i+7:i*8]}\n      );\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  always_comb begin\n    is_equal[3:0] = {4{is_equal_vec[3] & is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[1] & is_equal_vec[0]}};\n    is_greater[3:0] = {4{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & (is_greater_vec[2]\n                                            | (is_equal_vec[2] & (is_greater_vec[1]\n                                             | (is_equal_vec[1] & (is_greater_vec[0]))))))}};\n\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        is_equal[1:0]   = {2{is_equal_vec[0] & is_equal_vec[1]}};\n        is_equal[3:2]   = {2{is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[3]}};\n        is_greater[1:0] = {2{is_greater_vec[1] | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])}};\n        is_greater[3:2] = {2{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & is_greater_vec[2])}};\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        is_equal[3:0]   = is_equal_vec[3:0];\n        is_greater[3:0] = is_greater_vec[3:0];\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  logic [3:0] cmp_result;\n  logic d_eq, d_ne, d_gt, d_ge, d_lt, d_le;\n\n  assign d_eq = (operator_i == ALU_EQ);\n  assign d_ne = (operator_i == ALU_NE);\n  assign d_gt = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00010) & operator_i[3];  // GTS/GTU\n  assign d_ge = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00010) & ~operator_i[3]; // GES/GEU ... wait\n  assign d_lt = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0000) & ~operator_i[2] & ~operator_i[1];  // LTS/LTU/SLTS/SLTU\n  assign d_le = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0000) & operator_i[2] & ~operator_i[1];   // LES/LEU/SLETS/SLETU\n\n  assign cmp_result = ({4{d_eq}} & is_equal)\n                    | ({4{d_ne}} & ~is_equal)\n                    | ({4{d_gt}} & is_greater)\n                    | ({4{d_ge}} & (is_greater | is_equal))\n                    | ({4{d_lt}} & ~(is_greater | is_equal))\n                    | ({4{d_le}} & ~is_greater);\n\n  assign comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];\n\n  // min/max/abs\n  logic [31:0] result_minmax;\n  logic [ 3:0] sel_minmax;\n  logic        do_min;\n  logic [31:0] minmax_b;\n\n  assign minmax_b = (operator_i == ALU_ABS) ? abs_neg : operand_b_i;\n\n  assign do_min   = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00100) & operator_i[1];  // MIN/MINU/CLIP/CLIPU? no...\n\n  assign sel_minmax[3:0] = is_greater ^ {4{do_min}};\n\n  assign result_minmax[31:24] = (sel_minmax[3] == 1'b1) ? operand_a_i[31:24] : minmax_b[31:24];\n  assign result_minmax[23:16] = (sel_minmax[2] == 1'b1) ? operand_a_i[23:16] : minmax_b[23:16];\n  assign result_minmax[15:8] = (sel_minmax[1] == 1'b1) ? operand_a_i[15:8] : minmax_b[15:8];\n  assign result_minmax[7:0] = (sel_minmax[0] == 1'b1) ? operand_a_i[7:0] : minmax_b[7:0];\n\n  // clip\n  logic [31:0] clip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    clip_result = result_minmax;\n    if (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) begin\n      if (operand_a_i[31] || is_equal_clip) begin\n        clip_result = '0;\n      end else begin\n        clip_result = result_minmax;\n      end\n    end else begin\n      if (clip_cout || is_equal_clip) begin\n        clip_result = operand_b_neg;\n      end else begin\n        clip_result = result_minmax;\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Shuffle / pack\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic [3:0][1:0] shuffle_byte_sel;\n  logic [3:0]      shuffle_reg_sel;\n  logic [1:0]      shuffle_reg1_sel;\n  logic [1:0]      shuffle_reg0_sel;\n  logic [3:0]      shuffle_through;\n\n  logic [31:0] shuffle_r1, shuffle_r0;\n  logic [31:0] shuffle_r1_in, shuffle_r0_in;\n  logic [31:0] shuffle_result;\n  logic [31:0] pack_result;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    shuffle_reg_sel  = '0;\n    shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b01;\n    shuffle_reg0_sel = 2'b10;\n    shuffle_through  = '1;\n\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS: begin\n        if (operator_i == ALU_EXTS) shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b11;\n        if (vector_mode_i == shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0001;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0011;\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_PCKHI: begin\n        shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b00;\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          shuffle_through = 4'b1100;\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0100;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0011;\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_SHUF2: shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~operand_b_i[17];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] = ~operand_b_i[1];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] = ~operand_b_i[1];\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  always_comb begin\n    shuffle_byte_sel = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_EXTS, ALU_EXT: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n shuffle_byte_sel[0] = operand_b_i[1:0];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = {operand_b_i[16], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = {operand_b_i[16], ALU_INS: begin\n        shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b11;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b10;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b01;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b00;\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign shuffle_r0_in = shuffle_reg0_sel[1] ?\n                          operand_a_i :\n                          (shuffle_reg0_sel[0] ? {2{operand_a_i[15:0]}} : {4{operand_a_i[7:0]}});\n\n  assign shuffle_r1_in = shuffle_reg1_sel[1] ? {\n    {8{operand_a_i[31]}}, {8{operand_a_i[23]}}, {8{operand_a_i[15]}}, {8{operand_a_i[7]}}\n  } : (shuffle_reg1_sel[0] ? operand_c_i[7:0];\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Bit counting\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic [31:0] ff_input;\n  logic [ 5:0] cnt_result;\n  logic [ 5:0] clb_result;\n  logic [ 4:0] ff1_result;\n  logic        ff_no_one;\n  logic [ 4:0] fl1_result;\n  logic [ 5:0] bitop_result;\n  logic        a_is_zero;\n\n  assign a_is_zero = ~|operand_a_i;\n\n  cv32e40p_popcnt popcnt_i (\n      .in_i    (operand_a_i),\n      .result_o(cnt_result)\n  );\n\n  always_comb begin\n    ff_input = '0;\n    case (operator_i)\n      ALU_FF1: ff_input = operand_a_i;\n      7'b0110000, 7'b0110010, ALU_FL1: ff_input = operand_a_rev;  // DIVU, REMU, FL1\n      7'b0110001, 7'b0110011, ALU_CLB: begin  // DIV, REM, CLB\n        if .first_one_o(ff1_result),\n      .no_ones_o  (ff_no_one)\n  );\n\n  assign fl1_result = 5'd31 - ff1_result;\n  assign clb_result = ff1_result - 5'd1;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    bitop_result = '0;\n    case (operator_i)\n      ALU_FF1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_result};\n      ALU_FL1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, fl1_result};\n      ALU_CNT: bitop_result = = clb_result;\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Bit manipulation\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic extract_is_signed;\n  logic extract_sign;\n  logic [31:0] bmask_first, bmask_inv;\n  logic [31:0] bextins_and;\n  logic [31:0] div_shift_int + (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI(rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (shA_leftres),\n   .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO(div_ready)\n  );\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Result mux: one-hot AND-OR\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic g_and, g_or, g_xor, g_addfam, g_shift, g_bextins, g_bclr, g_bset, g_brev;\n  logic g_pack, g_minmax, g_abs, g_clip, g_vcmp, logic [3:0][1:0] shuffle_byte_sel;\n  logic [3:0]      shuffle_reg_sel;\n  logic [1:0]      shuffle_reg1_sel;\n  logic [1:0]      shuffle_reg0_sel;\n  logic [3:0]      shuffle_through;\n\n  logic [31:0] shuffle_r1, shuffle_r0;\n  logic [31:0] shuffle_r1_in, shuffle_r0_in;\n  logic [31:0] shuffle_result;\n  logic [31:0] pack_result;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    shuffle_reg_sel  = '0;\n    shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b01;\n    shuffle_reg0_sel = 2'b10;\n    shuffle_through  = '1;\n\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS: begin\n        if (operator_i == ALU_EXTS) shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b11;\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel[3:1] = 3'b111;\n          shuffle_reg_sel[0]   = 1'b0;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel[3:2] = 2'b11;\n          shuffle_reg_sel[1:0] = 2'b00;\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_PCKLO: begin\n        shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b00;\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          shuffle_through = 4'b0011;\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0001;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0011;\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_PCKHI: begin\n        shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b00;\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          shuffle_through = 4'b1100;\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0100;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0011;\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_SHUF2: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~operand_b_i[26];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~operand_b_i[18];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] = ~operand_b_i[10];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] = ~operand_b_i[2];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~operand_b_i[17];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~operand_b_i[17];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] = ~operand_b_i[1];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] = ~operand_b_i[1];\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_reg0_sel = 2'b00;\n            unique case (imm_vec_ext_i)\n              2'b00: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1110;\n              2'b01: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1101;\n              2'b10: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1011;\n              2'b11: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b0111;\n            endcase\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_reg0_sel   = 2'b01;\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] = imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] = imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  always_comb begin\n    shuffle_byte_sel = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_EXTS, ALU_EXT: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b0};\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      ALU_PCKLO: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: ;\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b01;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b00;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b01;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b00;\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      ALU_PCKHI: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: ;\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b11;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b10;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b11;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b10;\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      ALU_SHUF2, ALU_SHUF: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = operand_b_i[25:24];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = operand_b_i[17:16];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = operand_b_i[9:8];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = operand_b_i[1:0];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = {operand_b_i[16], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = {operand_b_i[16], 1'b0};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = {operand_b_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = {operand_b_i[0], 1'b0};\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b11;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b10;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b01;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b00;\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign shuffle_r0_in = shuffle_reg0_sel[1] ?\n                          operand_a_i :\n                          (shuffle_reg0_sel[0] ? {2{operand_a_i[15:0]}} : {4{operand_a_i[7:0]}});\n\n  assign shuffle_r1_in = shuffle_reg1_sel[1] ? {\n    {8{operand_a_i[31]}}, {8{operand_a_i[23]}}, {8{operand_a_i[15]}}, {8{operand_a_i[7]}}\n  } : (shuffle_reg1_sel[0] ? operand_c_i : operand_b_i);\n\n  assign shuffle_r0[31:24] = shuffle_byte_sel[3][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r0[23:16] = shuffle_byte_sel[2][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r0[15: 8] = shuffle_byte_sel[1][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r0[ 7:0] = shuffle_byte_sel[0][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n\n  assign shuffle_r1[31:24] = shuffle_byte_sel[3][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r1[23:16] = shuffle_byte_sel[2][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r1[15: 8] = shuffle_byte_sel[1][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r1[ 7:0] = shuffle_byte_sel[0][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n\n  assign shuffle_result[31:24] = shuffle_reg_sel[3] ? shuffle_r1[31:24] : shuffle_r0[31:24];\n  assign shuffle_result[23:16] = shuffle_reg_sel[2] ? shuffle_r1[23:16] : shuffle_r0[23:16];\n  assign shuffle_result[15:8] = shuffle_reg_sel[1] ? shuffle_r1[15:8] : shuffle_r0[15:8];\n  assign shuffle_result[7:0] = shuffle_reg_sel[0] ? shuffle_r1[7:0] : shuffle_r0[7:0];\n\n  assign pack_result[31:24] = shuffle_through[3] ? shuffle_result[31:24] : operand_c_i[31:24];\n  assign pack_result[23:16] = shuffle_through[2] ? shuffle_result[23:16] : operand_c_i[23:16];\n  assign pack_result[15:8] = shuffle_through[1] ? shuffle_result[15:8] : operand_c_i[15:8];\n  assign pack_result[7:0] = shuffle_through[0] ? shuffle_result[7:0] : operand_c_i[7:0];\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Bit counting\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic [31:0] ff_input;\n  logic [ 5:0] cnt_result;\n  logic [ 5:0] clb_result;\n  logic [ 4:0] ff1_result;\n  logic        ff_no_one;\n  logic [ 4:0] fl1_result;\n  logic [ 5:0] bitop_result;\n  logic        a_is_zero;\n\n  assign a_is_zero = ~|operand_a_i;\n\n  cv32e40p_popcnt popcnt_i (\n      .in_i    (operand_a_i),\n      .result_o(cnt_result)\n  );\n\n  always_comb begin\n    ff_input = '0;\n    case (operator_i)\n      ALU_FF1: ff_input = operand_a_i;\n      7'b0110000, 7'b0110010, ALU_FL1: ff_input = operand_a_rev;  // DIVU REMU FL1\n      7'b0110001, 7'b0110011, ALU_CLB: begin                      // DIV REM CLB\n        if (operand_a_i[31]) ff_input = ~operand_a_rev;\n        else ff_input = operand_a_rev;\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  cv32e40p_ff_one ff_one_i (\n      .in_i       (ff_input),\n      .first_one_o(ff1_result),\n      .no_ones_o  (ff_no_one)\n  );\n\n  assign fl1_result = 5'd31 - ff1_result;\n  assign clb_result = ff1_result - 5'd1;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    bitop_result = '0;\n    case (operator_i)\n      ALU_FF1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_result};\n      ALU_FL1: bitop_result = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, fl1_result};\n      ALU_CNT: bitop_result = cnt_result;\n      ALU_CLB: begin\n        if (ff_no_one) begin\n          if (operand_a_i[31]) bitop_result = 6'd31;\n          else bitop_result = '0;\n        end else begin\n          bitop_result = clb_result;\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Bit manipulation\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic extract_is_signed;\n  logic extract_sign;\n  logic [31:0] bmask_first, bmask_inv;\n  logic [31:0] bextins_and;\n  logic [31:0] bextins_result, bclr_result, bset_result;\n\n  assign bmask_first       = {32'hFFFFFFFE} << bmask_a_i;\n  assign bmask             = (~bmask_first) << bmask_b_i;\n  assign bmask_inv         = ~bmask;\n\n  assign bextins_and       = (operator_i == ALU_BINS) ? operand_c_i : {32{extract_sign}};\n\n  assign extract_is_signed = (operator_i == ALU_BEXT);\n  assign extract_sign      = extract_is_signed & shA_result[bmask_a_i];\n\n  assign bextins_result    = (bmask & shA_result) | (bextins_and & bmask_inv);\n\n  assign bclr_result       = operand_a_i & bmask_inv;\n  assign bset_result       = operand_a_i | bmask;\n\n  // bit reverse\n  logic [31:0] radix_2_rev;\n  logic [31:0] radix_4_rev;\n  logic [31:0] radix_8_rev;\n  logic [31:0] reverse_result;\n\n  generate\n    for (j = 0; j < 32; j++) begin : gen_radix_2_rev\n      assign radix_2_rev[j] = shA_result[31-j];\n    end\n    for (j = 0; j < 16; j++) begin : gen_radix_4_rev\n      assign radix_4_rev[2*j+1:2*j] = shA_result[31-j*2:31-j*2-1];\n    end\n    for (j = 0; j < 10; j++) begin : gen_radix_8_rev\n      assign radix_8_rev[3*j+2:3*j] = shA_result[31-j*3:31-j*3-2];\n    end\n    assign radix_8_rev[31:30] = 2'b0;\n  endgenerate\n\n  always_comb begin\n    reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n    unique case (bmask_a_i[1:0])\n      2'b01: reverse_result = radix_4_rev;\n      2'b10: reverse_result = radix_8_rev;\n      default: reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Divider\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  logic        div_signed;\n  logic        div_op_a_signed;\n  logic [ 5:0] div_shift_int;\n\n  assign div_signed = operator_i[0];\n\n  assign div_op_a_signed = operand_a_i[31] & div_signed;\n\n  assign div_shift_int = ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result;\n  assign div_shift = div_shift_int + (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI(rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (shA_leftres),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(a_is_zero),\n      .OpBSign_SI(div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO(result_div),\n      .InVld_SI (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO(div_ready)\n  );\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Result mux: one-hot AND-OR\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic g_and, g_or, g_xor, g_addfam, g_shift, g_bextins, g_bclr, g_bset, g_brev;\n  logic g_pack, g_minmax, g_abs, g_clip, g_vcmp, g_slt, g_bitcnt, g_subrot;\n\n  assign g_and    = (operator_i == ALU_AND);\n  assign g_or     = (operator_i == ALU_OR);\n  assign g_xor    = (operator_i == ALU_XOR);\n  assign g_addfam = is_addfam & ~is_subrot_i;\n  assign g_subrot = is_subrot_i;  // driven only together with ALU_SUB (16-bit mode)\n  assign g_shift  = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01001);\n  assign g_bextins = (operator_i == ALU_BEXT) || (operator_i == ALU_BEXTU) || (operator_i == ALU_BINS);\n  assign g_bclr   = (operator_i == ALU_BCLR);\n  assign g_bset   = (operator_i == ALU_BSET);\n  assign g_brev   = (operator_i == ALU_BREV);\n  assign g_pack   = (operator_i == ALU_SHUF) || (operator_i == ALU_SHUF2) ||\n                    (operator_i == ALU_PCKLO) || (operator_i == ALU_PCKHI) ||\n                    (operator_i == ALU_EXT) || (operator_i == ALU_EXTS) || (operator_i == ALU_INS);\n  assign g_minmax = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00100); // MIN MINU MAX MAXU\n  assign g_abs    = (operator_i == ALU_ABS);\n  assign g_clip   = (operator_i == ALU_CLIP) || (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU);\n  assign g_vcmp   = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0000 && operator_i[1] == 1'b0) ||\n                    (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0001);\n  assign g_slt    = (operator_i[6:3] == 4'b0000) && operator_i[1];  // SLTS/SLTU/SLETS/SLETU\n  assign g_bitcnt = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b01101);  // CNT CLB FF1 FL1\n\n  logic [31:0] abs_result;\n  assign abs_result = is_clpx_i ? {abs_neg[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]} : result_minmax;\n\n  logic [31:0] cmp_fill;\n  assign cmp_fill = {{8{cmp_result[3]}}, {8{cmp_result[2]}}, {8{cmp_result[1]}}, {8{cmp_result[0]}}};\n\n  assign result_o = ({32{g_and}} & (operand_a_i & operand_b_i))\n                  | ({32{g_or}}  & (operand_a_i | operand_b_i))\n                  | ({32{g_xor}} & (operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i))\n                  | ({32{g_addfam}} & shB_right)\n                  | ({32{g_subrot}} & shB_right)\n                  | ({32{g_shift}} & shA_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bextins}} & bextins_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bclr}} & bclr_result)\n                  | ({32{g_bset}} & bset_result)\n                  | ({32{g_brev}} & reverse_result)\n                  | ({32{g_pack}} & pack_result)\n                  | ({32{g_minmax}} & result_minmax)\n                  | ({32{g_abs}} & abs_result)\n                  | ({32{g_clip}} & clip_result)\n                  | ({32{g_vcmp}} & cmp_fill)\n                  | ({32{g_slt}} & {31'b0, cmp_result[3]})\n                  | ({32{g_bitcnt}} & {26'h0, bitop_result})\n                  | ({32{is_divop}} & result_div);\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ---- popcount ----\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  generate\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  generate\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  generate\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\nendmodule\n\n// ---- find first one ----\nmodule cv32e40p_ff_one #(\n    parameter LEN = 32\n) (\n    input logic [LEN-1:0] in_i,\n    output logic [$clog2(LEN)-1:0] first_one_o,\n    output logic                   no_ones_o\n);\n  localparam NUM_LEVELS = $clog2(LEN);\n  logic [          LEN-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_lut;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0]                 sel_nodes;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_nodes;\n\n  generate\n    genvar j;\n    for (j = 0; j < LEN; j++) begin : gen_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[j] = $unsigned(j);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    genvar l;\n    genvar level;\n    assign sel_nodes[2**NUM_LEVELS-1] = 1'b0;\n    for (level = 0; level < NUM_LEVELS; level++) begin : gen_tree\n      if (level < NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_non_root_level\n        for (l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : gen_node\n          assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+l]   = sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] | sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];\n          assign index_nodes[2**level-1+l] = (sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] == 1'b1) ?\n                                           index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] : index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];\n        end\n      end\n      if (level == NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_root_level\n        for (genvar kk = 0; kk < 2 ** level; kk++) begin : gen_node\n          if (kk * 2 < LEN - 1) begin : gen_two\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+kk] = in_i[kk*2] | in_i[kk*2+1];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+kk] = (in_i[kk*2] == 1'b1) ? index_lut[kk*2] : index_lut[kk*2+1];\n          end\n          if (kk * 2 == LEN - 1) begin : gen_one\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+kk]   = in_i[kk*2];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+kk] = index_lut[kk*2];\n          end\n          if (kk * 2 > LEN - 1) begin : gen_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+kk]   = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+kk] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign first_one_o = index_nodes[0];\n  assign no_ones_o   = ~sel_nodes[0];\nendmodule\n\n// ---- serial divider ----\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,\n    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,\n    input  logic                   InVld_SI,\n    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,\n    output logic                   OutVld_SO,\n    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO\n);\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n\n  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;\n  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;\n  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddTmp_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {\n    IDLE,\n    DIVIDE,\n    FINISH\n  }\n      State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpA_DI : BReg_DP;\n  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? AReg_DP : ResReg_DP_rev;\n  assign Res_DO = (ResInv_SP) ? -$signed(OutMux_D) : OutMux_D;\n  assign ABComp_S    = ((AReg_DP == BReg_DP) | ((AReg_DP > BReg_DP) ^ CompInv_SP)) & ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);\n  assign AddTmp_D = (LoadEn_S) ? 0 : AReg_DP;\n  assign AddOut_D = (PmSel_S) ? AddTmp_D + AddMux_D : AddTmp_D - $signed(AddMux_D);\n\n  assign Cnt_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBShift_DI : (~CntZero_S) ? Cnt_DP - 1 : Cnt_DP;\n  assign CntZero_S = ~(|Cnt_DP);\n\n  always_comb begin : p_fsm\n    State_SN   = State_SP;\n    OutVld_SO  = 1'b0;\n    LoadEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ARegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    BRegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ResRegEn_S = 1'b0;\n\n    case (State_SP)\n      IDLE: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (InVld_SI) begin\n          OutVld_SO = 1'b0;\n          ARegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          BRegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          LoadEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          State_SN  = DIVIDE;\n        end\n      end\n      DIVIDE: begin\n        ARegEn_S   = ABComp_S;\n        BRegEn_S   = 1'b1;\n        ResRegEn_S = 1'b1;\n        if (CntZero_S) begin\n          State_SN = FINISH;\n        end\n      end\n      FINISH: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n        if (OutRdy_SI) begin\n          State_SN = IDLE;\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign RemSel_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpCode_SI[1] : RemSel_SP;\n  assign CompInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBSign_SI : CompInv_SP;\n  assign ResInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? (~OpBIsZero_SI | OpCode_SI[1]) & OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(\n      OpA_DI\n  )] ^ OpBSign_SI) : ResInv_SP;\n\n  assign AReg_DN = (ARegEn_S) ? AddOut_D : AReg_DP;\n  assign BReg_DN = (BRegEn_S) ? BMux_D : BReg_DP;\n  assign ResReg_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? '0 : (ResRegEn_S) ? {\n    ABComp_S, ResReg_DP[$high(ResReg_DP):1]\n  } : ResReg_DP;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge Clk_CI or negedge Rst_RBI) begin : p_regs\n    if (~Rst_RBI) begin\n      State_SP   <= IDLE;\n      AReg_DP    <= '0;\n      BReg_DP    <= '0;\n      ResReg_DP  <= '0;\n      Cnt_DP     <= '0;\n      RemSel_SP  <= 1'b0;\n      CompInv_SP <= 1'b0;\n      ResInv_SP  <= 1'b0;\n    end else begin\n      State_SP   <= State_SN;\n      AReg_DP    <= AReg_DN;\n      BReg_DP    <= BReg_DN;\n      ResReg_DP  <= ResReg_DN;\n      Cnt_DP     <= Cnt_DN;\n      RemSel_SP  <= RemSel_SN;\n      CompInv_SP <= CompInv_SN;\n      ResInv_SP  <= ResInv_SN;\n    end\n  end\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v3.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
Great learning: v3's regression came from area + the bmask decode path. Now I'll write v4 with a comprehensive restructure:

1. **Unified Kogge-Stone prefix adder** (hand-written, 32-bit): replaces segmented adder + round adder; in 32-bit mode merges `a ± b + round` in ONE carry chain (CSA + one prefix tree). Handles ABS/CLIP/subrot via input muxes, boundary carry-kill/force for 16/8-bit lanes.
2. **Fast round decode**: `round[i] = (bmb ≤ i+1) & (bmb+bma ≥ i+1)` via thermometer decodes (~3 levels instead of two barrel shifts).
3. Keep v3's shA/shB split, one-hot result mux, dedicated subrot via unified adder.
