pk3[6], pk3[5], pk3[4], pk3[3], pk3[2], pk3[1], pk3[0]};\n\n  assign gk5 = {gk4[31] | (pk4[31] & gk4[15]), gk4[30] | (pk4[30] & gk4[14]),\n                gk4[29] | (pk4[29] & gk4[13]), gk4[28] | (pk4[28] & gk4[12]),\n                gk4[27] | (pk4[27] & gk4[11]), gk4[26] | (pk4[26] & gk4[10]),\n                gk4[25] | (pk4[25] & gk4[9]),  gk4[24] | (pk4[24] & gk4[8]),\n                gk4[23] | (pk4[23] & gk4[7]),  gk4[22] | (pk4[22] & gk4[6]),\n                gk4[21] | (pk4[21] & gk4[5]),  gk4[20] | (pk4[20] & gk4[4]),\n                gk4[19] | (pk4[19] & gk4[3]),  gk4[18] | (pk4[18] & gk4[2]),\n                gk4[17] | (pk4[17] & gk4[1]),  gk4[16] | (pk4[16] & gk4[0]),\n                gk4[15], gk4[14], gk4[13], gk4[12], gk4[11], gk4[10], gk4[9], gk4[8],\n                gk4[7], gk4[6], gk4[5], gk4[4], gk4[3], gk4[2], gk4[1], gk4[0]};\n\n  logic [31:0] adder_sum;\n  logic        adder_cout;\n  assign adder_cout = gk5[31];\n  assign adder_sum  = pa_t ^ pa_w ^ {gk5[30:0], pa_f0};\n\n  // rounding for 16/8-bit modes (full 32-bit add, carry crosses lanes)\n  logic [31:0] adder_round_result;\n  assign adder_round_result = (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE32) ? adder_sum :\n                                                              (adder_sum + round_value);\n\n  // CLIP overflow flag: carry out of the 32-bit a+b+1\n  logic clip_cout;\n  assign clip_cout = adder_cout;\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Shifter A: operand-fed (shift ops, bit-manip, div prep)\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic        shA_left;\n  logic        shA_arith;\n  logic [31:0] shA_amt, shA_amt_left, shA_amt_int, shA_op;\n  logic [31:0] shA_right, shA_leftres, shA_result;\n  logic [63:0] shA_op64;\n\n  assign shA_left = (operator_i == ALU_SLL) || (operator_i == ALU_BINS) ||\n                    (operator_i == ALU_BREV) || is_divop;\n  assign shA_arith = (operator_i == ALU_SRA) || (operator_i == ALU_BEXT);\n\n  assign shA_op = shA_left ? operand_a_rev : operand_a_i;\n  assign shA_amt = div_valid ? {26'b0, div_shift} : operand_b_i;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        shA_amt_left[15:0]  = shA_amt[31:16];\n        shA_amt_left[31:16] = shA_amt[15:0];\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        shA_amt_left[7:0]   = shA_amt[31:24];\n        shA_amt_left[15:8]  = shA_amt[23:16];\n        shA_amt_left[23:16] = shA_amt[15:8];\n        shA_amt_left[31:24] = shA_amt[7:0];\n      end\n      default: begin\n        shA_amt_left[31:0] = shA_amt[31:0];\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign shA_amt_int = shA_left ? shA_amt_left : shA_amt;\n\n  assign shA_op64 = (operator_i == ALU_ROR) ? {shA_op, shA_op} :\n                    {{32{shA_arith & shA_op[31]}}, shA_op};\n\n  always_comb begin\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        shA_right[31:16] = $signed({shA_arith & shA_op[31], shA_op[31:16]}) >>> shA_amt_int[19:16];\n        shA_right[15:0]  = $signed({shA_arith & shA_op[15], shA_op[15:0]}) >>> shA_amt_int[3:0];\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        shA_right[31:24] = $signed({shA_arith & shA_op[31], shA_op[31:24]}) >>> shA_amt_int[26:24];\n        shA_right[23:16] = $signed({shA_arith & shA_op[23], shA_op[23:16]}) >>> shA_amt_int[18:16];\n        shA_right[15:8]  = $signed({shA_arith & shA_op[15], shA_op[15:8]}) >>> shA_amt_int[10:8];\n        shA_right[7:0]   = $signed({shA_arith & shA_op[7], shA_op[7:0]}) >>> shA_amt_int[2:0];\n      end\n      default: begin\n        shA_right = shA_op64 >> shA_amt_int[4:0];\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  genvar j;\n  generate\n    for (j = 0; j < 32; j++) begin : gen_shA_left_result\n      assign shA_leftres[j] = shA_right[31-j];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign shA_result = shA_left ? shA_leftres : shA_right;\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Shifter B: adder-fed (adder family right shift)\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic        shB_arith;\n  logic [4:0]  shB_amt;\n  logic [31:0] shB_right;\n  logic [63:0] shB_op64;\n\n  assign shB_arith = ~operator_i[1];  // ADD/SUB/ADDR/SUBR arith; U variants logical\n  assign shB_amt = is_clpx_i ? {3'b000, clpx_shift_i} : bmask_b_i;\n\n  assign shB_op64 = {{32{shB_arith & adder_round_result[31]}}, adder_round_result};\n\n  always_comb begin\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        shB_right[31:16] = $signed({shB_arith & adder_round_result[31], adder_round_result[31:16]}) >>> shB_amt[3:0];\n        shB_right[15:0]  = $signed({shB_arith & adder_round_result[15], adder_round_result[15:0]}) >>> shB_amt[3:0];\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        shB_right[31:24] = $signed({shB_arith & adder_round_result[31], adder_round_result[31:24]}) >>> shB_amt[2:0];\n        shB_right[23:16] = $signed({shB_arith & adder_round_result[23], adder_round_result[23:16]}) >>> shB_amt[2:0];\n        shB_right[15:8]  = $signed({shB_arith & adder_round_result[15], adder_round_result[15:8]}) >>> shB_amt[2:0];\n        shB_right[7:0]   = $signed({shB_arith & adder_round_result[7], adder_round_result[7:0]}) >>> shB_amt[2:0];\n      end\n      default: begin\n        shB_right = shB_op64 >> shB_amt[4:0];\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Comparisons\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic [ 3:0] is_equal;\n  logic [ 3:0] is_greater;\n\n  logic [ 3:0] cmp_signed;\n  logic [ 3:0] is_equal_vec;\n  logic [ 3:0] is_greater_vec;\n  logic [31:0] operand_b_eq;\n  logic        is_equal_clip;\n\n  assign operand_b_eq = is_clipu ? '0 : operand_b_neg;\n  assign is_equal_clip = operand_a_i == operand_b_eq;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    cmp_signed = 4'b0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      7'b0001000, 7'b0001010, 7'b0000000, 7'b0000100,  // GTS GES LTS LES\n      7'b0000010, 7'b0000110,                           // SLTS SLETS\n      ALU_ABS, ALU_CLIP, ALU_CLIPU,\n      7'b0010000, 7'b0010010: begin                     // MIN MAX\n        case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8:  cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1111;\n          VEC_MODE16: cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1010;\n          default:    cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1000;\n        endcase\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : gen_is_vec\n      assign is_equal_vec[i] = (operand_a_i[8*i+7:8*i] == operand_b_i[8*i+7:i*8]);\n      assign is_greater_vec[i] = $signed(\n          {operand_a_i[8*i+7] & cmp_signed[i], operand_a_i[8*i+7:8*i]}\n      ) > $signed(\n          {operand_b_i[8*i+7] & cmp_signed[i], operand_b_i[8*i+7:i*8]}\n      );\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  always_comb begin\n    is_equal[3:0] = {4{is_equal_vec[3] & is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[1] & is_equal_vec[0]}};\n    is_greater[3:0] = {4{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & (is_greater_vec[2]\n                                            | (is_equal_vec[2] & (is_greater_vec[1]\n                                             | (is_equal_vec[1] & (is_greater_vec[0]))))))}};\n\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        is_equal[1:0]   = {2{is_equal_vec[0] & is_equal_vec[1]}};\n        is_equal[3:2]   = {2{is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[3]}};\n        is_greater[1:0] = {2{is_greater_vec[1] | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])}};\n        is_greater[3:2] = {2{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & is_greater_vec[2])}};\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        is_equal[3:0]   = is_equal_vec[3:0];\n        is_greater[3:0] = is_greater_vec[3:0];\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  logic [3:0] cmp_result;\n  logic d_eq, d_ne, d_gt, d_ge, d_lt, d_le;\n\n  assign d_eq = (operator_i == ALU_EQ);\n  assign d_ne = (operator_i == ALU_NE);\n  assign d_gt = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00010) & ~operator_i[1];  // GTS/GTU\n  assign d_ge = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00010) & operator_i[1];   // GES/GEU\n  assign d_lt = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00000);                   // LTS/LTU/SLTS/SLTU\n  assign d_le = (operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00001);                   // LES/LEU/SLETS/SLETU\n\n  assign cmp_result = ({4{d_eq}} & is_equal)\n                    | ({4{d_ne}} & ~is_equal)\n                    | ({4{d_gt}} & is_greater)\n                    | ({4{d_ge}} & (is_greater | is_equal))\n                    | ({4{d_lt}} & ~(is_greater | is_equal))\n                    | ({4{d_le}} & ~is_greater);\n\n  assign comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];\n\n  // min/max/abs\n  logic [31:0] result_minmax;\n  logic [ 3:0] sel_minmax;\n  logic        do_min;\n  logic [31:0] minmax_b;\n\n  assign minmax_b = is_abs ? adder_round_result : operand_b_i;\n\n  assign do_min   = ((operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00100) & ~operator_i[1]) |  // MIN/MINU\n                    ((operator_i[6:2] == 5'b00101) & operator_i[1]);    // CLIP/CLIPU\n\n  assign sel_minmax[3:0] = is_greater ^ {4{do_min}};\n\n  assign result_minmax[31:24] = (sel_minmax[3] == 1'b1) ? operand_a_i[31:24] : minmax_b[31:24];\n  assign result_minmax[23:16] = (sel_minmax[2] == 1'b1) ? operand_a_i[23:16] : minmax_b[23:16];\n  assign result_minmax[15:8] = (sel_minmax[1] == 1'b1) ? operand_a_i[15:8] : minmax_b[15:8];\n  assign result_minmax[7:0] = (sel_minmax[0] == 1'b1) ? operand_a_i[7:0] : minmax_b[7:0];\n\n  // clip\n  logic [31:0] clip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    clip_result = result_minmax;\n    if (is_clipu) begin\n      if (operand_a_i[31] || is_equal_clip) begin\n        clip_result = '0;\n      end else begin\n        clip_result = result_minmax;\n      end\n    end else begin\n      if (clip_cout || is_equal_clip) begin\n        clip_result = operand_b_neg;\n      end else begin\n        clip_result = result_minmax;\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Shuffle / pack\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  logic [3:0][1:0] shuffle_byte_sel;\n  logic [3:0]      shuffle_reg_sel;\n  logic [1:0]      shuffle_reg1_sel;\n  logic [1:0]      shuffle_reg0_sel;\n  logic [3:0]      shuffle_through;\n\n  logic [31:0] shuffle_r1, shuffle_r0;\n  logic [31:0] shuffle_r1_in, shuffle_r0_in;\n  logic [31:0] shuffle_result;\n  logic [31:0] pack_result;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    shuffle_reg_sel  = '0;\n    shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b01;\n    shuffle_reg0_sel = 2'b10;\n    shuffle_through  = '1;\n\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS: begin\n        if (operator_i == ALU_EXTS) shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b11;\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel[3:1] = 3'b111;\n          shuffle_reg_sel[0]   = 1'b0;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel[3:2] = 2'b11;\n          shuffle_reg_sel[1:0] = 2'b00;\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_PCKLO: begin\n        shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b00;\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          shuffle_through = 4'b0011;\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0001;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0011;\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_PCKHI: begin\n        shuffle_reg1_sel = 2'b00;\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          shuffle_through = 4'b1100;\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0100;\n        end else begin\n          shuffle_reg_sel = 4'b0011;\n        end\n      end\n      ALU_SHUF2: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~operand_b_i[26];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~operand_b_i[18];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] = ~operand_b_i[10];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] = ~operand_b_i[2];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~operand_b_i[17];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~operand_b_i[17];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] = ~operand_b_i[1];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] = ~operand_b_i[1];\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_reg0_sel = 2'b00;\n            unique case (imm_vec_ext_i)\n              2'b00: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1110;\n              2'b01: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1101;\n              2'b10: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b1011;\n              2'b11: shuffle_reg_sel[3:0] = 4'b0111;\n            endcase\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_reg0_sel   = 2'b01;\n            shuffle_reg_sel[3] = ~imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[2] = ~imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[1] = imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n            shuffle_reg_sel[0] = imm_vec_ext_i[0];\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  always_comb begin\n    shuffle_byte_sel = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_EXTS, ALU_EXT: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], ALU_PCKLO: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: ;\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b01;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b00;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b01;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b00;\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      ALU_PCKHI: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: ;\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b11;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b10;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b11;\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b10;\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      ALU_SHUF2, ALU_SHUF: begin\n        unique case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8: shuffle_byte_sel[0] = operand_b_i[1:0];\n          end\n          VEC_MODE16: begin\n            shuffle_byte_sel[3] = {operand_b_i[16], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[2] = {operand_b_i[16], 1'b0};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[1] = {operand_b_i[0], 1'b1};\n            shuffle_byte_sel[0] = {operand_b_i[0], 1'b0};\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        shuffle_byte_sel[3] = 2'b11;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[2] = 2'b10;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[1] = 2'b01;\n        shuffle_byte_sel[0] = 2'b00;\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign shuffle_r0_in = shuffle_reg0_sel[1] ?\n                          operand_a_i :\n                          (shuffle_reg0_sel[0] ? {2{operand_a_i[15:0]}} : {4{operand_a_i[7:0]}});\n\n  assign shuffle_r1_in = shuffle_reg1_sel[1] ? {\n    {8{operand_a_i[31]}}, {8{operand_a_i[23]}}, {8{operand_a_i[15]}}, {8{operand_a_i[7]}}\n  } : (shuffle_reg1_sel[0] ? operand_c_i : operand_b_i);\n\n  assign shuffle_r0[31:24] = shuffle_byte_sel[3][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[31:24] : shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r0[23:16] = shuffle_byte_sel[2][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] shuffle_r0_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r0_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r0_in[ 7: 0]);\n\n  assign shuffle_r1[31:24] = shuffle_byte_sel[3][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[3][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign (shuffle_byte_sel[2][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r1[15: 8] = shuffle_byte_sel[1][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[1][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n  assign shuffle_r1[ 7:0] = shuffle_byte_sel[0][1] ?\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[31:24] : shuffle_r1_in[23:16]) :\n                              (shuffle_byte_sel[0][0] ? shuffle_r1_in[15: 8] : shuffle_r1_in[ 7: 0]);\n\n  assign shuffle_result[31:24] = shuffle_reg_sel[3] ? shuffle_r1[31:24] : shuffle_r0[31:24];\n  assign shuffle_result[23:16] = shuffle_reg_sel[2] ? shuffle_r1[23:16] : shuffle_r0[23:16];\n  assign shuffle_result[15:8] = shuffle_reg_sel[1] ? shuffle_r1[15:8] : shuffle_r0[15:8];\n  assign shuffle_result[7:0] = shuffle_reg_sel[0] ? shuffle_r1[7:0] : [ASSISTANT]
Now let me rewrite the divider datapath to shorten the 1085ps sequential loop. The key: compute one subtractor `D = A-B` whose carry-out gives `A>=B` and zero-flag gives `A==B`, replacing the separate `==`, `>` and `-` trees, and make the AReg update mux shallow:

Original: ABComp = ((A==B) | ((A>B)^CompInv)) & gate\n  //   CompInv=0: (A==B)|(A>B) = (A>=B) = eqz | ~lt\n  //   CompInv=1: (A==B)|~(A>B) = (A<=B) = eqz | lt\n  logic [C_WIDTH:0] div_sub;\n  logic             div_lt, div_eqz;\n  assign div_sub  = {1'b0, AReg_DP} - {1'b0, BReg_DP};\n  assign div_lt   = div_sub[C_WIDTH];\n  assign div_eqz  = ~|div_sub[C_WIDTH-1:0];\n  assign ABComp_S = (div_eqz | ~(div_lt ^ CompInv_SP)) & ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);\n\n  assign AddTmp_D = (LoadEn_S) ? 0 : AReg_DP;\n  assign AddOut_D = (PmSel_S) ? AddTmp_D + AddMux_D : AddTmp_D - $signed(AddMux_D);")

## Summary

**Best result: `design_v2.sv` — cost 529007.56** (baseline 645629.32 → **18.1% reduction** in area×delay product), 100% functional correctness across 1,028,192 checks.

### Optimization journey
| Variant | Cost | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| starting_point.sv | 645629 | baseline |
| v1 | 606489 | one-hot AND-OR result mux (flattened the deep opcode→result case tree) + direct cmp_result decode |
| **v2 (best)** | **529007** | + split shifter: operand-fed shifter (shift/bit-manip/div-prep) vs adder-fed shifter (adder family). Removes the operator-decode chain from the worst path. |
| v3 | 603070 | dedicated ABS/CLIP/subrot adders + merged 3-op add — regressed (area + bmask decode path) |
| v4 | — | hand-written prefix adder, index bugs, failed |

### Key winning ideas in v2
1. **One-hot AND-OR result mux** — replaced the nested `unique case` (deep priority tree) with a flat 17-input `(sel & data) | ...` OR, shrinking the final stage.
2. **Split shifter datapath** — the adder family (ADD/SUB/±round/shift) and the operand-fed ops (SRA/SRL/ROR/SLL, bit-manip, div prep) got separate barrel shifters, so the opcode→shifter-operand mux no longer sits on the critical path.
3. **Operand_b_eq / rounding / cmp decodes** simplified to combinational expressions instead of `case`.

The divider's cycle-count and handshake behavior were preserved exactly (ready_o timing cycle-identical to reference), which the testbench verified.

Design complete.

Key optimizations that delivered the win:
1. **Flattened one-hot AND-OR result mux** replacing the deep nested `unique case` result tree (cut ~40ps of delay)
2. **Split shifter datapath**: separate operand-fed barrel shifter (shifts/bit-manip/div-prep) and adder-fed barrel shifter (adder family), decoupling opcode decode from the adder→round→shift chain
3. Simplified comparison/decode logic into direct combinational expressions

The divider's serial FSM, cycle counts, and handshake timing were kept exactly identical to the upstream reference, as required. All alternative attempts (merged prefix adder, dedicated ABS/CLIP adders, divider comparator/adder restructuring) were benchmarked and reverted when they scored worse.

## Session Summary

**Final: PASS, best cost 529,007 area_delay_product** (`design_v2.sv`) — 18.1% below baseline (645,629).

### 1. Approaches tried & what worked best
- **v1** (one-hot AND-OR result mux replacing the deep nested `unique case` tree, direct `cmp_result` decode): 645,629 → 606,489
- **v2** (+ split shifter: operand-fed `shA` for shifts/bit-manip/div-prep, adder-fed `shB` for adder family): → **529,007 (best)**
- v3 (dedicated ABS/CLIP/subrot adders, merged 3-op add): 603,071 — worse
- v4 (hand-written Kogge-Stone unified adder + thermometer round decode): failed correctness
- Divider loop rewrites (3 variants with shared subtractor replacing ==/>/−): 549,878–560,999 — all worse
- Thermometer round-constant decode on v2: 563,884 — worse, reverted

### 2. Highest-impact optimizations
1. **Split shifter datapath** (~72k cost reduction): removed the opcode→shifter-input mux from the critical path by giving the adder family its own right-shifter fed directly by the round-add result.
2. **Flattened result mux** (~39k): `(sel & data) | ...` one-hot AND-OR beats a priority case tree in both delay and mapping quality.
3. Simple combinational decode of `d_gt/d_ge/d_lt/d_le`, `addfam_neg = op[6:3]==0011 & op[0]`, etc.

### 3. What failed / regressed
- **Duplicating arithmetic (v3)**: dedicated ABS/CLIP adders added ~48 area units and created a new bmask-decode critical path — net loss despite shorter per-op paths.
- **Hand-written prefix adder (v4)**: three subtle bugs (wrong carry-prefix pair formulation, `{3'b000,clpx_shift}` vs upstream's implicit `bmask_b=clpx_shift` truncation to `{1'b0,clpx_shift}` in 32-bit mode, round-bit indexing) — cost many steps to debug, never beat v2.
- **Divider restructuring**: the serial loop (`AReg→cmp→AddOut→AReg`) at ~1086ps is the delay floor; every rewrite synthesized *worse* netlists. `target_delay` 300–1200ps had zero effect on it.
- Correctness traps found in v3: CLIP's adder computes `a+b+1` (not `a+~b+1`); `subrot` must flow through round+shift (bmask_b≠0 possible).

### 4. Lessons learned / next time
- **The critical path migrates**: after flattening the result mux, the divider's sequential loop became the floor (~1086ps × area). Further delay work on the combinational datapath was pointless; I should have pivoted earlier to **area reduction** (cost = area × delay, and delay is floored): e.g., cheaper shuffle/pack logic, sharing popcount's tree with ff_one, smaller divider registers/counters.
- **Keep one immutable best file** (`design_v2.sv`) and experiment via edit→evaluate→revert on the working copy; this avoided losing the best result several times.
- **Equivalence-checked refactors need exact semantics re-derivation**: upstream's segmented 36-bit adder with `1'b1` separators has subtle signed-extension semantics; copying structure verbatim first, then optimizing, is safer than re-deriving.
- Synthesis noise is small (~±20ps); single-run A/B comparisons were reliable here.
